- The temporary GDB setting was returned to disabled after the protocol probe. Do not attach a GDB client before the user has loaded the target state normally.

2026-07-14 - Intro E2 expanded v0.4 runtime rejection / v0.5 deferred skip
- v0.4 runtime result: FAIL. All expanded dialogues remained empty; following unmodified scenes and event progression were normal.
- Root cause: after processing a control, `0x8016BBA4` immediately reads `s0+0x14`. Lookup-time skipping moved it to `00 00`, causing `0x8016BBCC` to clear text state before the external pointer was rendered.
- v0.5 restores the byte-identical v3 lookup handler at `0x8018FCD0` and patches completion jump `0x8016BDC0` to helper `0x8018FD20`.
- The helper runs only when `s0+0x18` points to the secondary terminator. It checks the preceding inline E2 disk ID, loads slot metadata at `+0x7F`, advances `s0+0x14`, and resumes original completion target `0x8016BE44`.
- Independent disassembly: PASS. v3 lookup restored, completion deferred, custom-ID range guarded, original end pointer 13/13, external payload 13/13.
- Reproducibility: PASS. SHA256 `2BEEBDC203F92AD882DE1A9511F343FE7494E2EBAAD83BBADE9171E8B810B49F`.
- Runtime status: pending confirmation of visible expanded text, no blank pages, and normal scene continuation.

2026-08-05 | vertical glyph corruption, user report on six v129 save states | Anchored RAM and VRAM on the strips, confirmed all six states are v129 by the reserved block, extracted the mangled glyph's 12x12 ink mask from the framebuffer and searched the whole VRAM for its source | FAIL, cause found. The real bitmap is at x=1484, y=456 -- texture page (5,1), U=204, V=200. U and V are both correct; only the texture page is wrong.
- The font occupies VRAM y 0..467 at page x=5, which spans two texture pages vertically. V is `(row*12) & 0xFF`, which is right, but the page bit `(row*12) >> 8` is chosen by the classifier, and the classifier only knows the four strip V values 224/244/124/112. Any glyph on font rows 22..39 that is not a strip therefore samples page y=0 at the same U/V and draws twelve rows of some other cell.
- 25 characters are affected: 염영욕월익잎잔젤짜찍참창책척추축츠층칸컬켈콘클탄탈, on rows 31, 32, 33, 38 and 39. - Every one of the 25 has a correct twin in strip A (row 40) reachable by a two-byte EA code, so the repair is a same-width substitution: E6 AB->EA 60, E6 AC->EA 61, E6 AD->EA 62, E6 FE->EA 63, E7 01->EA 65, E7 02->EA 66, E7 53->EA 67, E7 54->EA 68, E8 EC->EA 6B, E8 ED->EA 6C, E8 EE->EA 6D, E8 EF->EA 6E, E8 F0->EA 6F, E8 F2->EA 71, E8 F3->EA 72, E8 F4->EA 73, E8 F5->EA 74, E8 F6->EA 75, E8 F7->EA 76, E8 F8->EA 77, E8 F9->EA 78, E8 FA->EA 79, E8 FC->EA 7B, E8 FD->EA 7C, E8 FE->EA 7E.

2026-08-05 | E2 slot terminator, whole-archive scan | Walked every non-empty external slot in every DAT of v129 and looked for a 0x00 in bytes 0..126 | FAIL. Seven slots have none: `21/S2021.DAT` slot 6, `5/S5031.DAT` slots 12, 13, 14 and 15, and `6/S6041.DAT` slots 0 and 1. `SLOT_TEXT_MAX` is 127, so a 127-byte payload fills bytes 0..126 and the builder then writes the `capacity - 2` metadata over byte 127, leaving the string with no terminator at all. The renderer reads on into the next slot. The correct limit is 126: text 0..125, terminator at 126, metadata at 127. This is the freeze the user hit in save state 6.

2026-08-06 | correction: the row budget was measured wrong | The user challenged a warning on `1/S1023.DAT 0x47C3C`, which renders correctly in game. Re-measured the wrap off the framebuffers instead of assuming | The 21-cell model was wrong twice. A space (0x9C) advances 6 pixels, not 12, so counting every token as one cell over-counts any line with spaces. And the window is not the original's row count: it is at least three rows.
- Width, measured: a row holds 228 pixels. In `21/S2041.DAT` the wrap breaks after 진행되 at exactly 228 and the next glyph would reach 240. In `1/S1061.DAT` the second row reaches 228 and the following space and glyph would reach 294. So the row is 228..239 pixels and 228 is the safe figure -- nineteen full-width glyphs.
- Height: `max(original rows, 3)`. The user's line has a two-row original, needs three rows of Korean and works. The ship line has a three-row original, needs four, and freezes. A floor of 4 would flag nothing at all and cannot be right, since the ship line does freeze.

2026-08-08 | 전투 세이브스테이트 두 장 VRAM 분석 | HASH-8679B1C6E71DE51C_1/_2 | COMM.IMG는 VRAM x 0~1791(4bpp) y 0~511에 그대로 올라간다. 이 상태의 VRAM 기준점은 GPU 구간 + 1329이고, 앵커는 글자 열(0~20)의 126바이트다. 스트립을 비운 빌드에서는 기존 extract_savestate_vram.py가 못 쓴다.
